<div class="v-main__wrap"><div class="container container--fluid"><div data-v-e441f948="" id="introduction" class="row docScroll"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e441f948="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> Introduction <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10">All API communications with Payfast are organized around REST. Our API has predictable resource-oriented URLs, returns JSON-encoded responses and uses standard HTTP response codes and verbs.</div><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p>
                  </p><h3>Account Setup:</h3><br>
                  Login to your merchant account and make sure that the following is setup:<br>
                  <ul>
                    <li>A passphrase as set on the settings page <em>(see <a href="/api#authentication" class="link--text">Authentication</a> below)</em></li>
                    <li>Payfast merchant ID</li>
                    <li>The version of the API you want to interact with</li>
                  </ul><br>
                   <h3>IP Whitelisting</h3><br>
                   <p>Each request to the Recurring Billing API may be controlled by an IP address whitelist. If set, the IP address of the server completing the API call must be in the given list, else the action will not be processed. The whitelist can be set on the merchant’s settings page and is an optional security setting.</p>
                   <p>Go to <b>RECURRING BILLING</b> and edit the <b>IP Whitelist</b>.</p></div><!----><div data-v-3114aa33="" data-v-9b0f0344="" role="alert" class="v-alert v-sheet v-sheet--outlined theme--light v-alert--outlined v-alert--text info--text"><div class="v-alert__wrapper"><i aria-hidden="true" class="v-icon notranslate v-alert__icon mdi mdi-information theme--light info--text"></i><div class="v-alert__content"><span data-v-3114aa33="">The Payfast API and integration can be tested through the <a href="https://sandbox.payfast.co.za/" target="_blank" class="link--text">sandbox</a>.<br>
You can also use Payfast's <a href="https://documenter.getpostman.com/view/10608852/TVCmSQZu" class="link--text" target="_blank">POSTMAN API collection</a>.</span></div></div></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e441f948="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text">BASE URL</span>  </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><!----><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-html" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-html">https://api.payfast.co.za </code></pre></div></div></div></div><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10">Outline of a JSON formatted response:</div><!----><div data-v-0fc80144="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mx-auto mb-4 v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey lighten-5"><div class="v-system-bar theme--dark blue-grey lighten-4" style="height: 24px;"><div class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 class="blue-grey--text text--darken-3 font-weight-bold text-uppercase"> response </h5></div></div><div class="v-card__text"><div data-v-0fc80144="" class="vjs-tree" path="res"><div class="vjs-tree-list"><div class="vjs-tree-list-holder"><div class="vjs-tree-list-holder-inner"><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><!----></div><!----><span><span class="vjs-tree-brackets">{</span><!----><!----></span></div><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><div class="vjs-indent-unit has-line"></div><!----></div><span class="vjs-key">"code"<span>:</span></span><span><span class="vjs-value vjs-value-number">200</span><span>,</span><!----></span></div><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><div class="vjs-indent-unit has-line"></div><!----></div><span class="vjs-key">"status"<span>:</span></span><span><span class="vjs-value vjs-value-string">"success"</span><span>,</span><!----></span></div><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><div class="vjs-indent-unit has-line"></div><!----></div><span class="vjs-key">"data"<span>:</span></span><span><span class="vjs-tree-brackets">{</span><!----><!----></span></div><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><div class="vjs-indent-unit has-line"></div><!----></div><!----><span><span class="vjs-tree-brackets">}</span><!----><!----></span></div><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><!----></div><!----><span><span class="vjs-tree-brackets">}</span><!----><!----></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><ul><li><span class="response-heading-2--text">code</span>: The HTTP status code for the result</li><li><span class="response-heading-2--text">status</span>: A more verbose description of the result</li><li><span class="response-heading-2--text">data</span>: Will contain a response of either true or false for the action and may also contain one or more description(s) for the reason of the result</li></ul></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e441f948="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><h3>Request body</h3><br>Variables may be sent via both the headers and body of each request as specified.<br>Sending data to the API may be done in one of two ways:<p></p>

                <ul>
                    <li>
                        <b>application/x-www-form-urlencoded</b><br>
                        Essentially one big query string where all name / value pairs are separated by the ‘&amp;’ symbol, with an equal (=) symbol between name and value. This is similar to submitting the traditional HTML FORM via POST (eg. frequency=3&amp;cycles=12…).<br><br>
                    </li>
                    <li>
                        <b>application/json</b><br>
                        Fields should be passed in as a single-level JSON representation.<br><em>See the JSON request example</em>
                    </li>
                </ul>

                </div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e441f948="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text">JSON Request</span>  </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><!----><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json"><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token property">"frequency"</span> <span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">3</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span><span class="token property">"cycles"</span> <span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">12</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span> </code></pre></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e441f948="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><h3>Request Timestamp</h3><br>
                <p>Each request to the API must contain an ISO-8601 formatted timestamp. This field must be present in each request body along with the signature. Failure to include this field will result in an error. The timestamp field must be formatted according to ISO-8601 standards, and be in the following format:</p>
                <p><strong>YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM[(+-)HH:MM] (e.g. 2020-04-01T12:00:01+02:00)</strong></p>
                <p>The time zone offset group ((+-)HH:MM) is optional. If not included, the default will be assumed to be GMT +2.</p></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e441f948="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text">Request timestamp</span>  </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><!----><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json">timestamp<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">2020</span><span class="token number">-02</span>-01T12<span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token number">00</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token number">01</span>+<span class="token number">02</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token number">00</span> </code></pre></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-e441f948="" class="col"><hr data-v-e441f948="" role="separator" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="ma-3 v-divider theme--light"><div data-v-45f5acb6="" data-v-e441f948="" id="ping-response" class="row section docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-45f5acb6="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> Ping API response <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">Used to check if the API is responding to requests.</p></div><!----><div data-v-77146efc="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mb-8"><h3 data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pb-4"> Parameters </h3><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">merchant-id</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer, 8 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the Merchant ID as given by the Payfast system.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">version</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the Payfast API version (i.e. v1).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">timestamp</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">ISO-8601 date and time</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the current timestamp (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS[+HH:MM]).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">signature</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, MD5 hash of the alphabetised submitted header and body variables, as well as the passphrase. Characters must be in lower case.</div><!----></div></div></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-45f5acb6="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-2 blue--text">GET</span> /ping </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><!----><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json">https<span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token comment">//api.payfast.co.za/ping </span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">Live response:</p></div><!----><div data-v-0fc80144="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mx-auto mb-4 v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey lighten-5"><div class="v-system-bar theme--dark blue-grey lighten-4" style="height: 24px;"><div class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 class="blue-grey--text text--darken-3 font-weight-bold text-uppercase"> response </h5></div></div><div class="v-card__text"><div data-v-0fc80144="" class="vjs-tree" path="res"><div class="vjs-tree-list"><div class="vjs-tree-list-holder"><div class="vjs-tree-list-holder-inner"><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><!----></div><!----><span><span class="vjs-value vjs-value-string">"Payfast API"</span><!----><!----></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">Sandbox response:</p></div><!----><div data-v-0fc80144="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mx-auto mb-4 v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey lighten-5"><div class="v-system-bar theme--dark blue-grey lighten-4" style="height: 24px;"><div class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 class="blue-grey--text text--darken-3 font-weight-bold text-uppercase"> response </h5></div></div><div class="v-card__text"><div data-v-0fc80144="" class="vjs-tree" path="res"><div class="vjs-tree-list"><div class="vjs-tree-list-holder"><div class="vjs-tree-list-holder-inner"><div class="vjs-tree-node" collapsed-on-click-brackets="true"><!----><!----><div class="vjs-indent"><!----></div><!----><span><span class="vjs-value vjs-value-string">"API V1"</span><!----><!----></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-2844dcb0="" id="authentication" class="row docScroll"><div data-v-58edd572="" data-v-2844dcb0="" class="header-block mb-3 col col-12"><div data-v-58edd572="" style="display: flex; align-items: center;"><!----><h1 data-v-58edd572=""> Authentication </h1></div><p data-v-58edd572="" class="main--text" style="margin-left: 40px;"><p>Data integrity is ensured through a signature together with passphrase passed in the header of all requests.</p></p></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-2844dcb0="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><h3 class="mt-3 mb-3">Merchant Passphrase</h3>
                <p>The passphrase is used to salt the signature (as described below).<br>The passphrase is considered a secret between the merchant and Payfast and should never be sent or given out.<br><br>
                The merchant may set their own passphrase by:
                </p><ol>
                    <li>Login to Payfast using their merchant credentials.</li>
                    <li>Clicking on "Settings", and then "Edit" under the <em><b>Security Pass Phrase</b></em> section.</li>
                    <li>Inputting the desired passphrase and click "Update"</li>
                </ol></div><!----><div data-v-3114aa33="" data-v-9b0f0344="" role="alert" class="v-alert v-sheet v-sheet--outlined theme--light v-alert--outlined v-alert--text warning--text"><div class="v-alert__wrapper"><i aria-hidden="true" class="v-icon notranslate v-alert__icon mdi mdi-exclamation theme--light warning--text"></i><div class="v-alert__content"><span data-v-3114aa33="">A <b>passphrase</b> must be set by the merchant before any API interaction is allowed</span></div></div></div><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><h3 class="mt-3 mb-3">Signature Generation:</h3>

                <p>A generated MD5 signature must be passed in the header of the request and this hash is used to ensure the integrity of the data transfer.<br><br>
                <b>Signature generation steps</b>
                </p><ol>
                    <li>Sort all the submitted variables (header, body, query string parameters and passphrase) in <b>alphabetical</b> order.<br>NOTE: When in test mode the <b>testing</b> parameter should be excluded from the signature.</li>
                    <li>Create a string by concatenating all the non-empty, sorted variables, with ‘&amp;’ used as a separator.<br>Each value should also be urlencoded (eg. “merchant-id=10000100&amp;passphrase=​passphrase&amp;..&amp;version=v1”)</li>
                    <li>Make sure there is no trailing '&amp;'</li>
                    <li>MD5 hash your string.</li>
                </ol></div><!----><div data-v-3114aa33="" data-v-9b0f0344="" role="alert" class="v-alert v-sheet v-sheet--outlined theme--light v-alert--outlined v-alert--text warning--text"><div class="v-alert__wrapper"><i aria-hidden="true" class="v-icon notranslate v-alert__icon mdi mdi-exclamation theme--light warning--text"></i><div class="v-alert__content"><span data-v-3114aa33=""><b>Variable order:</b> The pairs must be listed in <b>alphabetical order</b> eg. email_address=john@doe.com&amp;​name_first=John&amp;name_last=Doe<br><em>* Do not use the <b>custom payment signature</b> format, which requires pairs to be listed in the order in which they appear in the documentation!</em></span></div></div></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-2844dcb0="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text">Signature generation</span>  </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-input selector theme--dark v-text-field v-text-field--single-line v-text-field--solo v-text-field--solo-flat v-text-field--is-booted v-text-field--enclosed v-select"><div class="v-input__control"><div role="button" aria-haspopup="listbox" aria-expanded="false" aria-owns="list-263" class="v-input__slot"><div class="v-select__slot"><label for="input-263" class="v-label theme--dark" style="left: 0px; right: auto; position: absolute;">Select library</label><div class="v-select__selections"><input id="input-263" readonly="readonly" type="text" aria-readonly="false" autocomplete="off"></div><div class="v-input__append-inner"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mdi mdi-unfold-more-horizontal"></span></div><input type="hidden" value="Select library"></div><div class="v-menu"><!----></div></div><div class="v-text-field__details"><div class="v-messages theme--dark"><div class="v-messages__wrapper"></div></div></div></div></div><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-php" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-php"><span class="token php language-php"><span class="token delimiter important">&lt;?php</span>
<span class="token comment">/**
 * Generate signature for API
 * @param array $pfData (all of the header, body and query string param values to be sent to the API)
 * @param null $passPhrase
 * @return string
 */</span>
<span class="token keyword">function</span> <span class="token function-definition function">generateApiSignature</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token variable">$pfData</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token variable">$passPhrase</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token constant">null</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>

    <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token variable">$passPhrase</span> <span class="token operator">!==</span> <span class="token constant">null</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="token variable">$pfData</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string single-quoted-string">'passphrase'</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token variable">$passPhrase</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span>

    <span class="token comment">// Sort the array by key, alphabetically</span>
    <span class="token function">ksort</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token variable">$pfData</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

    <span class="token comment">//create parameter string</span>
    <span class="token variable">$pfParamString</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token function">http_build_query</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token variable">$pfData</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
    <span class="token keyword">return</span> <span class="token function">md5</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token variable">$pfParamString</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span> </span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-43f87a06="" id="errors" class="row docScroll"><div data-v-58edd572="" data-v-43f87a06="" class="header-block mb-3 col col-12"><div data-v-58edd572="" style="display: flex; align-items: center;"><!----><h1 data-v-58edd572=""> Errors </h1></div><p data-v-58edd572="" class="main--text" style="margin-left: 40px;"><p>Payfast uses conventional HTTP response codes to indicate the type of failure of an API request.</p></p></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-43f87a06="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p>In general: Codes in the 4xx range indicate an error that failed given the information provided (e.g., a required parameter was omitted, a charge failed, etc.).&nbsp;</p>
<p>Some 4xx errors that could be handled programmatically (e.g., a card is declined) include an error code that briefly explains the error reported.</p>
<p>Codes in the 5xx range indicate a Payfast networking or application error.</p></div><!----><div data-v-77146efc="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mb-8"><h3 data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pb-4"> data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey lighten-5"><div class="v-system-bar theme--dark blue-grey lighten-4" style="height: 24px;"><div class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 class="blue-grey--text text--darken-3 font-weight-bold text-uppercase"> ERROR CODES </h5></div></div><div class="v-card__text"><div class="v-data-table table-bg-transparent theme--light"><div class="v-data-table__wrapper"><table><tbody class="blue-grey--text text--darken-2"><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">400 - Required variables not present in request</td><td>The request is missing some or all of the required fields.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">400 - Value for signature is not in the expected format</td><td>Generated when the merchant signature provided is not an MD5 hash, or is malformed.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">400 - API version is not valid</td><td>Issued when the version header refers to a version that does not exist, or is malformed.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">400 - Signature not present in headers</td><td>The signature has not been provided.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">401 - Merchant not found</td><td>The given merchant ID was not found.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">401 - Request origin not recognised</td><td>Generated when the request does not contain a REMOTE_ADDR value.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">401 - Request origin could not be verified</td><td>Occurs when the request is made from a location not specified in the whitelist.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">401 - Merchant authorisation failed</td><td>The signature is incorrect.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">404 - Service / endpoint not found</td><td>The endpoint does not exist or the requesting merchant does not have permission to access.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">429 - Signature rate limit reached, please try again in a few minute</td><td>The rate limit was hit.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">500 - Application Error</td><td>Application Error.</td></tr><tr><td class="font-weight-bold">500 - Communication Failure</td><td>Networking issue with the API.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-efd59a50="" id="recurring-billing" class="row docScroll"><div data-v-58edd572="" data-v-efd59a50="" class="header-block mb-3 col col-12"><div data-v-58edd572="" style="display: flex; align-items: center;"><!----><h1 data-v-58edd572=""> Recurring Billing </h1></div><p data-v-58edd572="" class="main--text" style="margin-left: 40px;"><p>The Subscription Payments API gives Merchants the ability to interact with subscriptions on their accounts.</p></p></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-efd59a50="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">
                <b>URLs to interact with take the following form:</b><br>
                https://api.payfast.co.za/subscriptions/​[token]/[action]<br><br>
                <b>Sandbox:</b><br>
                https://api.payfast.co.za/subscriptions/​[token]/[action]?testing=true</p>
                <p><b>Related Guide:</b> <a href="/docs#recurring_billing" class="link--text">Recurring Billing</a></p></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-efd59a50="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text">Endpoints</span>  </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><!----><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-html" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-html">GET /subscriptions/:token/fetch
PUT /subscriptions/:token/pause
PUT /subscriptions/:token/unpause
PUT /subscriptions/:token/cancel
PATCH /subscriptions/:token/update
POST /subscriptions/:token/adhoc
 </code></pre></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-efd59a50="" class="col"><hr data-v-efd59a50="" role="separator" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="ma-3 v-divider theme--light"><div data-v-77c01522="" data-v-efd59a50="" id="subscription-object-fetch" class="row section docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-77c01522="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> The subscription object <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">This is the object representing your subscription.<br>You can retrieve it to see the details of your subscription.</p></div><!----><div data-v-77146efc="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mb-8"><h3 data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pb-4"> Parameters </h3><div Payfast system.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-1172833b="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> Pause a subscription <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">Should a subscription be paused the remaining payments (cycles) will remain untouched. The end date of the subscription moves on by the number of paused frequency period(s). Effectively the customer gains a payment gap and the number of payments will still be the same as originally requested. A free month(s) could be provided by pausing (pause) a subscription and reducing the number of cycles by making an update to the subscription.</p></div><!----><div data-v-77146efc="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mb-8"><h3 data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pb-4"> Parameters </h3><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col class="vjs-tree-brackets">}</span><!----><!----></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-129b8aa8="" data-v-efd59a50="" id="create-an-ad-hoc-subscription" class="row docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-129b8aa8="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> Charge a tokenization payment <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">Charge a tokenization payment based on the token provided.<br>When receiving the API call to charge a customer's credit card via a token, Payfast will attempt to charge the card immediately. If the credit card is out of funds a failed response will be returned.</p></div><!----><div data-v-77146efc="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mb-8"><h3 data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pb-4"> Parameters </h3><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">token</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Path</b>, the Unique ID on Payfast that represents the subscription.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">merchant-id</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer, 8 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the Merchant ID as given by the Payfast system.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">version</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the Payfast API version (i.e. v1).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">timestamp</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">ISO-8601 date and time</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the current timestamp (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS[+HH:MM]).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">signature</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, MD5 hash of the alphabetised submitted header and body variables, as well as the passphrase. Characters must be in lower case.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">amount</span></span><span which the buyer must pay, in <b>cents</b> (ZAR), no decimals.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the name of the item being charged for.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">item_description</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string, 255 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-optional">OPTIONAL</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the description of the item being charged for.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">itn</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">boolean</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 default).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span </h1></div><p data-v-58edd572="" class="main--text" style="margin-left: 40px;"><p>The transaction history API gives Merchants the ability to interact with their Payfast account.</p></p></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-07d44a62="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p>URLs to interact with take the following form:<br>
                https://api.payfast.co.za/transactions/​history/[timeframe]</p></div></div><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-07d44a62="" class="col-md-6 col-12 mt-2 pa-6"><!----><!----><div data-v-8eb20f98="" data-v-9b0f0344=""><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" data-v-8eb20f98="" class="mb-4"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="mx-auto v-card v-sheet theme--light blue-grey darken-3"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-system-bar theme--light blue-grey darken-4" style="height: 24px;"><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-list-item__title text-wrap py-2"><h5 data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text"><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text">Endpoints</span>  </h5></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="spacer"></div><!----><span data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-tooltip v-tooltip--top"><!----></span><button data-v-01e6e6eb="" type="button" on="[object Object]" attrs="[object Object]" class="v-icon notranslate text--lighten-3 blue-grey--text v-icon--link mdi mdi-file-multiple-outline theme--light"></button></div><div data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="v-card__text"><pre data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json" tabindex="0"><code data-v-01e6e6eb="" class="language-json">GET /transactions/history?from=<span class="token operator">:</span>date&amp;to=<span class="token operator">:</span>date
GET /transactions/history/daily?date=<span class="token operator">:</span>date
GET /transactions/history/weekly?date=<span class="token operator">:</span>date
GET /transactions/history/monthly?date=<span class="token operator">:</span>date </code></pre></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-07d44a62="" class="col"><hr data-v-07d44a62="" role="separator" token</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">funding_type</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">The payment method used in the original transaction</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">amount_original</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" amount</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">amount_available_for_refund</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">The remaining amount if partial refunds have already been performed on the instruction.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">status</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">The refund status, one of: REFUNDABLE, COMPLETED, NOT_AVAILABLE</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">errors</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">array string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-optional">OPTIONAL</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">If status is <b>NOT_AVAILABLE</b> the errors array will contain a message to display to the end user</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">refund_full</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">object</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">An object containing the parameters required for <a href="api#refund-create" class="link--text">creating the full refund</a>.<br>
                   <b>method:</b> one of: PAYMENT_SOURCE, BANK_PAYOUT, NOT_AVAILABLE</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">refund_partial</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">object</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">An object containing the parameters required for <a href="api#refund-create" class="link--text">creating the partial refund</a>.<br>
                    <b>method:</b> one of: PAYMENT_SOURCE, BANK_PAYOUT, NOT_AVAILABLE</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">bank_names</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">array</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text">When a bank refund is possible a list of available banks will be provided, this will allow you populate the <b>bank_name</b> accurately when processing a refund via a bank payout.</div><!----></div></div></div></div></div><div data-v-2e95bfce="" data-v-6bbb99d4="" id="refund-create" class="row section docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-2e95bfce="" class="col-md-6 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> Create a refund <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10"><p class="mt-2">Create a new refund.</p></div><!----><div data-v-3114aa33="" data-v-9b0f0344="" role="alert" class="v-alert v-sheet v-sheet--outlined theme--light v-alert--outlined v-alert--text info--text"><div class="v-alert__wrapper"><i aria-hidden="true" class="v-icon notranslate v-alert__icon mdi mdi-information theme--light info--text"></i><div class="v-alert__content"><span data-v-3114aa33="">Before creating a new refund, make sure to call the <a href="api#refund-query" class="link--text">query refund endpoint</a>, this will provide you with all the necessary information for creating a refund.<br>
                This information includes amounts available for refund, if a refund is possible, which payment method can be used for the refund and any additional parameters that are required for that refund payment method.</span></div></div></div><!----><div data-v-77146efc="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="mb-8"><h3 data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pb-4"> Parameters </h3><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">id</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Path</b>, The <b>pf_payment_id</b> (Unique transaction ID on Payfast)</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">merchant-id</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer, 8 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the Merchant ID as given by the Payfast system.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">version</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the Payfast API version (i.e. v1).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">timestamp</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">ISO-8601 date and time</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, the current timestamp (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS[+HH:MM]).</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">signature</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Header</b>, MD5 hash of the alphabetised submitted header and body variables, as well as the passphrase. Characters must be in lower case.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">amount</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the amount to refund in <b>cents</b> (ZAR).<br>Both partial and full amounts are supported.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">reason</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string, 3-255 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the reason for the refund.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">notify_merchant</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">boolean, 1 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-optional">OPTIONAL</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, Whether or not to notify the merchant of the refund. 1 = true, 0 = false.<br>Default is 0.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">notify_buyer</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">boolean, 1 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, Whether or not to notify the buyer of the refund. 1 = true, 0 = false.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">acc_holder | bank_account_holder</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string, 255 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED - BANK_PAYOUT ONLY</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the name of the account holder.</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">bank_name</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">string, 255 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED - BANK_PAYOUT ONLY</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the name of the bank. The list is available via <a href="api#refund-query" class="link--text">query refund endpoint</a></div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">bank_branch_code</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer, max 6 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED - BANK_PAYOUT ONLY</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the bank branch code</div><!----></div></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="row mx-0"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="border-bottom pl-0 mb-2 col col-12"><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mb-3 mainS--text"><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-4 item-name v-chip v-chip--label v-chip--no-color theme--light v-size--default"><span class="v-chip__content">bank_account_number</span></span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mr-2 item-type">integer, max 12 char</span><span data-v-77146efc="" class="mx-2 item-required">REQUIRED - BANK_PAYOUT ONLY</span></div><div data-v-77146efc="" class="mainS--text"><b>Body</b>, the bank account number</div><!----></div></div><div class="row section docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-e4caa5b4="" class="col-md-12 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> 4. Incidents <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10">Incidents are the cornerstone of any status page, being composed of many incident updates. <br>Each incident usually goes through a progression of statuses listed below, with an impact calculated from a blend of component statuses (or an optional override). <br><br><p><strong>Status:</strong> <em>Investigating, Identified, Monitoring, Resolved, or Postmortem</em></p><p><strong>Impact:</strong> <em>None (black), Minor (yellow), Major (orange), or Critical (red)</em></p></div></div></div><div data-v-6c8c433d="" data-v-a5b4d9b4="" id="system-status-unresolved-incidents" class="row section docScroll mt-6"><div data-v-9b0f0344="" data-v-6c8c433d="" class="col-md-12 col-12 pa-6"><h2 data-v-9b0f0344=""> 4.1 Unresolved Incidents <!----></h2><!----><div data-v-8074d680="" data-v-9b0f0344="" class="main--text mb-10">Get a list of any unresolved incidents. This endpoint will only return incidents in the Investigating, Identified, or Monitoring state.</div></div><div data-v-a5ca412c="" data-v-6c8c433d="" class="px-6 col-md-12 col-12"><div
